**Source note (written in Phase 3).** Until Phase 3 there was no phase record in the repository,
and none of the work is committed (git history ends at `e413bf0 documentation`, which predates
Phase 1; all phase work is in the working tree). The Phase 1 and Phase 2 sections below are
therefore written from what the current code and tests verify, plus the Phase 1 and Phase 2
implementation reports given at the end of those phases. Nothing here is inferred beyond those

## Phase 1 — Mascot Reliability

### Objective
Aadhi (the mascot) must never be unintentionally static during a lesson, and must follow the
narration and scene playback.

### Baseline found
Five full-screen position clips in `video_template/` (left, right, center, popup, empty studio),
switched by CSS class in `index.html`; `play()` failures only logged; no handling of `error`,
`stalled`, `waiting` or `ended`; only metadata preloaded; no fallback; generated scenes using
`"popup"` or capitalised positions silently hid Aadhi; narration `[SYNC]`/`[PAUSE]` did not affect
the mascot; blocked narration audio was retried every 100 ms and blocked browser speech hung.
There are no per-state (talking/idle) clips in the project.

### Implementation
- `mascot.js`: one `MascotController` owning every mascot `<video>` layer.
  - Behaviour states `idle`, `talking`, `explaining`, `thinking`, `question`, `success`
    (fallback is a separate display mode, not a state); same-state changes are no-ops.
  - Placement (`aadhi_position`) selects the clip; `resolvePlacement()` keeps the old layout rules
    and repairs `"popup"` and capitalised values.
  - Per-lesson preloading (8 s cap), load retries at 1 s and 3 s, crossfade only once the incoming
    clip has a frame, one playing clip enforced by a 1 s watchdog, restart on `ended`, stall
    detection (3.5 s without progress).
  - Fallback: the clip's poster frame (new `video_template/posters/*.jpg`, frame 0 of each clip)
    with CSS breathing/sway and a talking indicator; last resort the empty-studio still.
  - Narration drives state: segment start → talking, `[SYNC]` reveal → explaining, `[PAUSE]` →
    thinking, hold/end → idle; quiz checkpoints → question, thinking (countdown), success.
    Optional scene field `mascot_state` (backward compatible), added to the generation prompt.
  - `PlaybackGate`: one "Enable playback" button when the browser blocks media; recording mode;
    debug HUD (`?mascotDebug=1`).
- `index.html` integration; `server.py` route for `/mascot.js`.

### Files changed
Created `mascot.js`, `video_template/posters/*.jpg`, `tests/mascot.test.js`,
`tests/helpers/fake-dom.js`, `tests/mascot_browser_check.mjs`, `tests/fixtures/mascot_lesson.json`.
Modified `index.html`, `server.py`, `README.md`.

### Tests
- Unit (Node `node:test`, fake DOM): 24/24.
- Real-browser check (Chrome via playwright-core): 27/27 — every state and placement, broken clip
  → animated fallback, built-in demo lesson (no `aadhi_position`), Enable-playback click.

### Issues found and fixed during the phase
Playback-gate button rendered off-screen (a later `.btn-gold` rule overrode `position: fixed`);
cue bubble floating unattached in the popup framing (removed there); misleading fallback reason for
failed clips.

### Known limitations
No per-state clips exist, so talking/explaining look alike while a clip plays (the controller
switches to state clips if added with `data-state`); no blinking for photoreal stills; a real
autoplay block cannot be triggered under automation (simulated in the check); Safari/Firefox not
tested; white frames in headless screenshots during View Transitions (pre-existing, also on the
original page); cue positions hand-measured from the current clips.

### Final status
Complete with limitations (as reported at the end of Phase 1).

## Phase 2 — Reliable Video Recording / Export

### Objective
Turn a lesson into a complete video that is persisted on the server, downloadable later, with
visible progress, retries and understandable errors.

### Baseline found
Two recorders (● manual, ⚡ "Ultimate Export") using `getDisplayMedia` + `MediaRecorder` (WebM VP9,
15 Mbit/s); the whole video held in memory as one chunk and downloaded through an immediately
revoked object URL; nothing uploaded or stored; "Default (Edge-TTS)" narration actually used the
browser's `speechSynthesis`, which tab capture cannot record; simulations and AI videos generated
while recording; opening a project from History reloaded the page forever.

### Implementation
- `video_exports` table (`models.VideoExport`) and `exports.py` router: create job, status
  transitions enforced server-side, resumable chunked upload (8 MB PUT at byte offsets, streamed to
  disk), validation (magic bytes + ffprobe), lossless remux for duration/seek index, silence
  detection (audio track never above −60 dB counts as no sound), storage at
  `exports/<user>/<id>.<ext>`, list/get, 30-minute signed links for preview/download, authenticated
  download with byte ranges, conservative cleanup.
- `export.js`: API client, `LessonRecorder` (validates the shared stream, 1 s timeslice, file built
  only in `onstop`, 1.5 s tail), `ExportFlow` (prepare → capture → record → upload → save), panel
  with progress, preview, download, retry, history.
- `index.html`: asset preparation before recording, narration through server Edge-TTS while
  exporting, cached simulation videos, History-reload fix, "My Videos" buttons.
- `server.py`: `/save-history` returns the project id; scoped (link) tokens are rejected as logins.

### Files changed
Created `exports.py`, `export.js`, `tests/test_exports.py`, `tests/export.test.js`,
`tests/export_e2e.mjs`, `tests/fixtures/export_lesson.json`. Modified `index.html`, `server.py`,
`models.py`, `README.md`, `.env.example`, `.gitignore`.

### Tests
Backend 16/16; frontend unit 43/43 (24 Phase 1 + 19 export); Phase 1 browser check 27/27;
end-to-end 30/30 (login, record the whole lesson in headless Chrome, upload, persist, refresh,
preview and seek, download, cancelled share, second-account isolation, ffprobe/ffmpeg inspection of
the downloaded file: VP9 + Opus, audible narration, every scene present).

### Issues found and fixed during the phase
Playwright's default `--mute-audio` made the test recording silent (test harness); "My Videos"
button hidden under the page title; export panel layered under the control bar and subtitles.

### Known limitations
End-to-end ran in headless Chrome with an auto-accepted share dialog; Chrome/Edge only;
real-time recording (about 1.4 GB for 12 minutes at 15 Mbit/s); recording held in memory until
uploaded; signed links are bearer links for 30 minutes; storage is local disk only (Railway and
Hugging Face disks are wiped on restart without a volume); `JWT_SECRET` should be set.

### Final status
Complete with limitations (as reported at the end of Phase 2).

## Phase 3 — Asset Library

### Objective
Give lesson media a reusable home: register each image, sound or video once, give it a stable ID
and metadata, let any lesson and the video exporter refer to it by that ID, and know where it is
used — instead of treating every file as a disposable URL owned by one lesson or export.

### Source / Baseline
Built on Phase 2: `exports.py` (jobs, resumable upload, validation, signed links, download,
cleanup, ownership checks), `video_exports` table, files under `exports/<user>/<id>.webm`,
`export.js` recording flow. Baseline at the start of Phase 3: backend 16/16, frontend unit 43/43.

### Audit
- **Existing assets.** All generated media lands in `static_videos/`, named by content hash of the
  request: Manim renders `<Scene>_<hash>.mp4` (`/render`), AI videos `ai_video_<hash>.mp4`
  (`/generate-ai-video`), narration `audio_<hash>.mp3|wav` (`/generate-audio`, keyed by
  text+voice+engine), uploads `<uuid8>_<client name>` (`/upload-media`), DOCX images
  `img_<uuid>.png` (`/upload-image`). Side-panel images come from `/get-image` (Pollinations, cached
  in `images/`, route has no login).
- **Storage.** Local disk only. `static_videos/`, `images/` and `video_template/` are served by
  **public** mounts (`/static`, `/images`, `/video_template`) with no login. Exports (Phase 2) are
  private and authenticated.
- **Lessons.** Scenes refer to media only by URL: `video_url` (ai_video), `manim_video_url`
  (simulation, Phase 2 cache), `side_panel.video_url` (side Manim), `uploaded_images[imgId]`, and
  `<img src>` in `html`. Narration is never stored in a lesson (re-fetched by hash).
- **Mascot.** Five position clips, five posters, background still, intro logo video and music in
  `video_template/`, referenced directly by `index.html`, shared by everyone, never copied.
- **Manim / AI video.** Cached server-side by content hash (not regenerated when the code or prompt
  is unchanged); no metadata beyond the file.
- **Exports.** Phase 2 already validated media with ffprobe and had its own path helpers and
  signed-link code.
- **Nothing recorded** ownership, type, size, duration, content hash, or which lessons used a file.
- **Security finding.** `/upload-media` built its path from the client's file name; on Windows a
  name such as `/../../x` resolves outside `static_videos/` (path traversal). Fixed in this phase.

### Architecture
- **Asset model** (`assets` table, `models.Asset`): stable random ID (uuid4 hex); `scope_key`
  (`system` or `user:<id>`) and `owner_id` (NULL = system asset); `kind` (video, audio, image);
  `source` (mascot, narration, manim, ai-video, upload, system); `status`; display `file_name`;
  sniffed `mime_type`; `storage_volume` + server-generated `storage_key` (never sent to clients);
  `file_size`, `sha256`, `duration_seconds`, `width`, `height`, `has_audio`; `details` (JSON:
  voice/engine/text for narration, scene for Manim, prompt for AI video, placement for mascot);
  `error_message`; timestamps incl. `deleted_at`. Unique on (scope, volume, key).
- **References** (`asset_references`): one row per asset, saved lesson (project) and place in the
  lesson (e.g. `scenes[3].video_asset_id`), recorded whenever a lesson is saved.
- **Storage abstraction** (`storage.py`, `LocalStorage`): the only code that turns keys into paths;
  rejects absolute paths, `..`, backslashes, drive letters and empty segments. Phase 2 exports were
  refactored onto it, so there is one storage engine. Volumes: `assets` (`ASSETS_DIR`, uploads,
  stored as `blobs/<sha[:2]>/<sha>.<ext>`), `static` (`static_videos/`), `system`
  (`video_template/`). No object storage was added.
- **Registration.** Upload → size limit → type from the file's own bytes + ffprobe (`media.py`,
  shared with exports) → SHA-256 (streamed) → deduplicate → store → row. Existing generator output
  and shipped system files are **adopted in place** (no copy); unchanged files are not re-read.
- **Deduplication.** By content hash, never by name. Same content for the same owner = the same
  asset. Same content for another owner = a separate asset row pointing at the one stored file.
  Physical files are shared across volumes too (an upload identical to a generated file reuses it).
- **Access control.** Private assets: owner only (others get 404, same as unknown). System assets:
  readable by every signed-in user, not deletable. File content only through
  `GET /api/assets/{id}/content` with a login token or a 12-hour signed link scoped to that asset
  (`access.py`, shared with exports); link tokens are not logins. `nosniff`, inline disposition
  with a sanitised name, byte ranges.
- **Lifecycle.** `pending` (reserved for generators that finish later), `ready`, `failed` (file
  missing or invalid; set automatically when a file disappears), `deleted` (soft). Registering the
  same content again repairs a failed asset or revives a deleted one with the same ID.
- **References and safe deletion.** Only your own or shared assets are recorded for your lesson
  (another user cannot pin your asset). Delete is refused (409) while any saved lesson uses the
  asset; otherwise soft delete, and the physical file is removed only if it lives in the managed
  `assets` volume and no other asset uses it. Files in `static_videos/` and `video_template/` are
  never deleted by the library.

### Implementation
- `assets.py`: `AssetLibrary` (register, adopt, adopt_url, attach, resolve, references, delete),
  router, system-asset registration at startup (13 files: 5 clips, 5 posters, background still,
  intro video, music), `display_name()` sanitiser.
- `storage.py`, `media.py`, `access.py` (shared building blocks; `exports.py` now uses them).
- `server.py`: library setup and router; thin wrappers around `/render`, `/generate-ai-video`,
  `/generate-audio`, `/upload-media`, `/upload-image` that add `asset_id` to the existing responses
  (registration can never break a generator); `/save-history` records references (asset IDs,
  `asset:` markers, and plain `/static/...` URLs of older lessons, adopted for the owner);
  `/upload-media` path-traversal fix; `/assets.js` route.
- `assets.js`: `AssetApi`, lesson resolver (`collectAssetIds`, `applyResolved`, `resolveLesson`),
  `AssetLibraryPanel`.
- Lesson references (all optional, backward compatible): `video_asset_id`, `manim_asset_id`,
  `side_panel.video_asset_id`, `uploaded_image_assets[imgId]`, `<img src="asset:ID">` in `html`.
  The resolver writes short-lived URLs into the fields the renderer already reads and keeps a
  `data-asset-src` marker so a lesson can be re-resolved when links expire.
- `index.html`: lessons are resolved before the first scene (alongside the mascot preload) and at
  the start of export preparation (missing assets are reported before recording); every place that
  stores a generated or uploaded media URL now also stores the returned `asset_id`.

### API
| Endpoint | Purpose |
|---|---|
| `POST /api/assets` | Upload a file (multipart); validated, deduplicated; returns the asset and `deduplicated` |
| `GET /api/assets` | Your assets and shared system assets; filters `kind`, `source`, `scope` (`mine`/`system`), `q` (name), `status`; paginated |
| `GET /api/assets/{id}` | One asset with its reference count and the saved lessons (yours) that use it |
| `DELETE /api/assets/{id}` | Delete your unused asset (403 system, 409 in use, 404 not yours) |
| `POST /api/assets/resolve` | Asset IDs → 12-hour content URLs; unusable IDs listed in `missing` |
| `GET /api/assets/{id}/content` | The file (login token or signed link); 410 if the file is missing |

`PATCH` was not added: nothing in the project edits asset metadata yet.

### Frontend
Asset Library panel (🗂 **Assets** on the start screen): type/source filters, name search, list
with shared badges, preview (image, audio player, video player), metadata (type, source, access,
size, duration, resolution, sound, status, "used by", content hash, ID), upload with progress and
duplicate notice, copy asset ID, copy image tag (`<img src="asset:ID">`), delete (disabled while
used), loading/empty/error states, Escape to close, one-column layout on phones. The app has one
(dark) theme, so there is no light mode to support. A per-scene asset picker in the lesson editor
was not built (IDs can be pasted into the script editor).

### Files changed
- **Created:** `storage.py`, `media.py`, `access.py`, `assets.py`, `assets.js`,
  `tests/backend_env.py`, `tests/test_assets.py`, `tests/assets.test.js`,
  `tests/assets_browser_check.mjs`, `all-phases-details.md`.
- **Modified:** `exports.py` (onto shared storage/media/access; behaviour unchanged), `server.py`,
  `models.py`, `index.html`, `README.md`, `.env.example`, `.gitignore` (`/assets/`),
  `tests/test_exports.py` (shared test setup), `tests/export_e2e.mjs` (silent audio, lesson opened
  without playing, `E2E_USE_ASSETS=1` mode, scene timing note), `tests/mascot_browser_check.mjs`
  (silent audio; crossfade rule checked directly instead of as a sample percentage).

### Tests
- **Backend** (`unittest` + FastAPI TestClient, one throwaway DB/storage shared by all modules):
  16 export tests (Phase 2) + 23 asset tests — registration and metadata (image/audio/video),
  server-chosen storage paths, invalid files (text, MIME-spoofed HTML, SVG with script, empty,
  truncated MP4), dedup by content not name, cross-user shared file, listing and filters, system
  assets shared and undeletable, cross-user isolation (get/delete/content/resolve), signed links
  (scope, expiry, forged user, not a login), safe content headers and ranges, missing file (410 →
  failed → repaired), unknown IDs, deletion (file removed only when unshared), referenced asset kept
  (409), foreign references ignored, reference scanning of every lesson field incl. legacy URLs,
  generator output adopted in place and not re-read, `/upload-media` traversal, path safety,
  performance (300 assets listed/resolved without reading files).
- **Frontend unit** (`node:test`): 24 mascot + 19 export + 7 asset-resolver/API tests.
- **Asset Library browser check** (real Chrome): 19 checks — library opens with shared clips,
  filters, video/image/audio previews, upload, duplicate upload recognised, search, copy ID, delete,
  empty search state, used asset locked, server-error state, Escape, a lesson's `asset:` image and
  `video_asset_id` clip render in the player, second account sees only shared assets and cannot
  open/resolve yours, phone layout, no console/page/network errors.
- **Export regression:** Phase 2 end-to-end unchanged, and again with `E2E_USE_ASSETS=1` (the
  lesson's picture and AI clip referenced by asset ID only; checks that saving records both
  references, that a used asset cannot be deleted, and that the recording used the library URLs).
- **Phase 1 regression:** mascot browser check.
- All browser tests run silently since this phase (see Issues).

### Results
Final clean QA run (after all fixes, server restarted fresh):

| Suite | Result |
|---|---|
| Static checks (6 inline scripts, 4 JS files, 7 Python modules parse) | pass |
| Backend unit (`unittest`) | **39/39** |
| Frontend unit (`node --test`) | **50/50** |
| Phase 1 mascot browser check (silent) | **27/27** |
| Asset Library browser check (silent) | **19/19** |
| Phase 2 export end-to-end (silent) | **29/30** — failed: "Aadhi never still for 1 s" (1.56 s) |
| Export end-to-end with library assets (silent) | **34/35** — failed: same check (2.04 s) |

Earlier in this phase, before the silent test configuration, the same application code passed the
export end-to-end 30/30 (twice) and the library-asset variant 35/35. The failing check is an extra
smoothness metric on the recorded file; every mandatory flow item passed in every run (record,
upload, persist, refresh, preview, seek, download, cancelled share, second-account isolation, VP9 +
Opus, audible narration, text, Aadhi on screen, image, chart, Manim video, AI video, quiz, final
scene, scenes in order). Other results: server restart with the library takes about 2 s and
registers no duplicates (system files are only stat'ed after the first start); resolving 300
assets is one query; existing data (users, projects, video_exports) intact after the new tables
were added by `create_all`.

### Issues found and fixed during the phase
- `/upload-media` path traversal from the client file name (security).
- Uploads identical to a generated file were stored twice (cross-volume dedup added).
- `POST /api/assets/resolve` committed once per asset (3.2 s for 300); batched.
- Lesson resolver re-matched its own `data-asset-src` marker on a second resolve.
- Export preparation preloaded the `asset:` marker instead of the image URL (greedy regex).
- Panel: nested metadata list failed to render; delete confirmation overwritten by "Loading…";
  Escape ignored when focus had left the panel; start-screen button row did not wrap.
- Test harness: several browser tests could be heard on the machine running them (the export test
  ran Chrome unmuted to record tab audio, and browser speech bypasses Chrome's mute). All browser
  tests now disable speaker output (`--disable-audio-output`), stub browser speech, and the export
  test captures with `suppressLocalAudioPlayback`; a probe confirmed the recording still gets the
  sound.

### Known limitations
- **Test environment / smoothness metric.** In the final silent runs the recorded mascot area was
  still for 1.6–2.8 s once (below Phase 1's 3.5 s stall threshold, so no fallback yet). These runs
  shared the CPU with a heavy, unrelated machine-learning job running on the same machine, and used
  the new silent configuration (no audio output device, which may affect playback timing in
  headless Chrome). The same code passed this check in all four earlier Phase 3 runs. Not observed
  as a code regression; not verified in a normal (headed, GPU) Chrome.
- The same load made recordings longer than in Phase 2 (a 3-second simulation clip played in bursts
  over about 27 s; each scene still waits for its video, so nothing is cut). Media routes answer in
  milliseconds.
- Storage is still local disk; production persistence is **not** solved (needs a volume or a future
  object-storage implementation of `LocalStorage`).
- The public `/static`, `/images` and `/video_template` mounts remain public for older lessons; the
  library's own content route is authenticated. Signed content links are bearer links (12 hours).
- `/get-image` results (side-panel images by prompt) are not registered: that route has no login.
- No garbage collection of unused library files; projects can never be deleted in this app, so
  references accumulate (a used asset stays protected).
- The start screen is not usable on phones (taller than the screen, cannot scroll; pre-existing);
  the library panel itself is responsive.
- Pre-existing, unrelated: no favicon (404); "Logo video play error AbortError" logged when the
  intro is skipped while starting.

### Deferred to Later Phases
- **Phase 4 (Visual Router):** choosing/reusing library assets per scene; side-panel image sourcing
  (`/get-image` by prompt) and registering those images.
- **Phase 5 (AI caching):** generator caching is unchanged (hash-named files in `static_videos/`);
  the library records them and reserves the `pending` status for asynchronous generation.
- **Phase 7:** recording at 60 fps VP9 is CPU heavy; a 30 fps or re-encode option belongs to the
  video pipeline work.
- **Phase 8:** provider details are only stored as metadata (`details`).
- **Phase 9:** cleanup of orphaned library files and failed assets; recovery of missing files.
- **Phase 10:** Manim rendering untouched.
- Not phase-assigned: production object storage (implement `LocalStorage`'s interface for it);
  tightening the public media mounts once older lessons are migrated; a per-scene asset picker in
  the lesson editor.

### Final status
**COMPLETE WITH LIMITATIONS.** The Asset Library, its API, UI, lesson and export integration are
implemented; backend 39/39, frontend unit 50/50, Phase 1 check 27/27 and Asset Library check 19/19
pass; the Phase 2 export flow and the library-asset export flow pass every mandatory item. One
smoothness check on the recorded mascot failed in the final runs under the conditions described in
Known limitations. Nothing was committed, pushed or deployed.

## Phase 4 — Visual Router

### Objective
One central place decides where each scene's visual comes from, so AI generation is the last
resort instead of the default ("Gemini is a visual provider, not the visual engine"): the asset a
lesson names, media it already has, a matching library asset, a built-in renderer or Manim, and
only then AI media. Planning is separate from generating, AI can be switched off, and the preview
and the video export show the same visuals. Built on the Phase 3 Asset Library (no second asset
system).

### Audit (visual flow before Phase 4)
```
screenplay scene ──► index.html renderSlide / updateSidePanel ──► provider, called directly
  ai_video.prompt              ──► POST /generate-ai-video  (Veo via Gemini API, LTX, or manual)   on every preview of a scene without video_url
  side_panel {type:image}      ──► GET /get-image?prompt=   (Pollinations; no login)               preloaded for ALL image panels at lesson start
  simulation.manim_code        ──► POST /render             (local Manim, cached by code)
  side_panel {type:manim}      ──► POST /render
  side_panel chart/graph/3d/terminal/animation/quiz/skill_tree ──► Chart.js / JSXGraph / Three.js / DOM (built in)
  side_panel {type:gif}        ──► GET /get-gif             (Giphy search)
  p5_simulation.p5_code        ──► p5.js iframe
  html <img src>, asset:ID, video_asset_id, manim_asset_id, uploaded_image_assets ──► Phase 3 resolver
export: prepareLessonForExport generated every missing AI video and image before recording.
```
Findings: nothing ever looked in the Asset Library before generating; every preview or export of
an unfinished AI scene could call a paid provider; AI images came from Pollinations (not Gemini);
generated AI videos were already registered in the library with their prompt (Phase 3), but never
reused by a later lesson.

### Architecture
```
LESSON SCREENPLAY ──► requests_from_scenes() ──► VisualRequest per slot
                                                  │  (main visual, side panel, each asset:ID / <img>, uploaded image)
                                                  ▼
                        VisualRouter: fixed chain of small rules, first answer wins
   1 ExplicitAssetRule   video_asset_id / manim_asset_id / side_panel.video_asset_id / asset:ID / uploaded_image_assets
                         usable → ASSET or SYSTEM_ASSET; unusable → NONE + error (never substituted)
   2 ExistingMediaRule   media the scene already has: /api/assets/<id>/content = that asset; /static file checked
                         on disk; external URL → UPLOADED_ASSET. A gone file keeps routing (an <img> reports it)
   3 PreviousMatchRule   an asset matched earlier stays chosen while usable (preview == export)
   4 BuiltInRendererRule the scene carries data for chart / graph / 3d_model / terminal / animation /
                         skill_tree / quiz / p5 / svg → PROCEDURAL; gif → EXTERNAL_MEDIA
   5 ManimRule           the scene has Manim code → MANIM (existing /render pipeline)
   6 NoVisualRule        quiz, text, "none" intent; an equation intent with TeX in the scene → PROCEDURAL mathjax
   7 LibraryMatchRule    deterministic match against your + shared assets (threshold, next candidate on failure)
   8 AiGenerationRule    AI_VIDEO (veo / ltx / manual / offline) or AI_IMAGE (pollinations), as a plan only;
                         AI off → NONE with would_require
                                                  ▼
                        VisualPlan {slot, source, media, selection, asset_id, url, renderer, provider,
                                    requires_generation, would_require, score, reason, error, debug}
                                                  ▼
             page stores main/side plans in scene.visual_plan (saved with the lesson)
                     │                                             │
                  PREVIEW                                       EXPORT
      renders from the plan; generates only       replans (earlier choices kept), prepares only
      what the "AI Visuals" setting allows        what the setting allows, warns about the rest
```
- `VisualSource` enum: ASSET, SYSTEM_ASSET, UPLOADED_ASSET, PROCEDURAL, MANIM, EXTERNAL_MEDIA
  (GIF search, not generated), AI_IMAGE, AI_VIDEO, NONE. `MediaType`: STATIC_IMAGE, VIDEO,
  ANIMATION, INTERACTIVE, NONE.
- The priority order was adapted to the code: media a scene already has (older URLs, generated
  files) is kept before any library match, so existing lessons never change what they show; the
  built-in renderers only apply when the scene carries their data, so they cannot hide a library
  asset for a scene that asks for a photo or clip.
- **Library matching** (`asset_profile`, `request_profile`, `match_score`): words from the request
  (concept ×2, keywords ×2, description ×1) against words from the asset's metadata (keywords 1.0,
  concepts 1.0, description / generation prompt 0.85, scene / role 0.6, placement 0.5, Aadhi words
  for shared mascot pictures 0.6, file name 0.35). Score = weighted coverage + 0.15 when the
  request's concept appears as a phrase, capped at 1. Stopwords, camelCase, simple stemming and
  negation ("no_aadhi", "without people") are handled. A file name alone can never reach the
  default threshold (`VISUAL_MATCH_THRESHOLD`, 0.6). Images only match image slots and videos
  video slots. Ties: own asset before shared, larger, newer. One database query per plan loads the
  candidates; files are only stat'ed for assets actually chosen and for a scene's own `/static`
  media (no directory scans, no decoding).
- **Side panels** get library images or videos, and generated images only (never an AI video).
  `preferred_media_type` decides image or video for side visuals whose intent names neither.
- **Controls:** `allow_ai_generation`, `prefer_existing_assets`, `prefer_procedural`,
  `preferred_media_type` per request; `AI_GENERATION_ENABLED=0` switches every AI media call off on
  the server (`/generate-ai-video` and uncached `/get-image` answer 403) and plans say what would
  have been needed.
- **Page setting "AI Visuals"** (setup screen, per browser): *Auto: images* (default: AI images
  automatically, AI videos only when the user clicks "Generate AI video"), *Auto: images + videos*,
  *Off*. Planning never generates in any mode.
- **Screenplay:** optional `visual` intent (`concept`, `description`, `keywords`, `type`:
  image / video / diagram / chart / graph / equation / none) added to the generation prompt; Gemini
  describes the visual, the router decides. Scenes without it route exactly as before.
- **References:** the plan call carries the open lesson's `project_id`; the chosen library assets
  are recorded as used by that lesson (`scenes[i].visual_plan.<slot>`, one row per slot, replaced
  when the choice changes, never duplicated). Saving the lesson rescans `visual_plan` too.

### Implementation
- `visuals.py` (new): requests, enums, rules, router, one-query candidate loading, reference
  recording, `POST /api/visuals/plan`.
- `visuals.js` (new): page client — AI Visuals setting, plan options, `shouldAutoGenerate`,
  storing plans in scenes, source labels, debug rows, API client.
- `index.html`: plans the lesson after resolving library assets (presentation start and export
  preparation); AI-video, simulation, side image, side Manim and "visual"-intent scenes render from
  the plan (library clip, "Visual unavailable" card with an explicit replace/re-render button, "This
  scene needs an AI video — Generate" card); side images are preloaded only after planning and only
  when allowed; export preparation generates only what the setting allows and warns otherwise;
  source badge per scene in the scene list (info panel); `?visualDebug=1` prints each decision
  with `console.table`.
- `assets.py`: `PATCH /api/assets/{id}` (description, keywords for matching); `visual_plan`
  references.
- `server.py`: router wiring, AI kill switch on both AI generators, `/visuals.js`.

### API
| Endpoint | Purpose |
|---|---|
| `POST /api/visuals/plan` | Plans every visual of a lesson; never generates. Body: `scenes`, the four controls, optional `project_id` (must be yours; 404 otherwise), `debug`. Returns `plans`, `summary`, `ai_generation_enabled`, `match_threshold`. 413 over 400 scenes, 422 bad `preferred_media_type`, 401 without login. |
| `PATCH /api/assets/{id}` | Describe your asset: `description` (≤1000 chars), `keywords` (list, ≤30, ≤60 chars each); whitespace tidied, repeated keywords dropped (case-insensitive), empty clears, a field left out is unchanged, other details kept. 422 over a limit, 403 shared, 404 not yours / deleted. (Limits as revised by the follow-up below.) |
| `POST /generate-ai-video`, `GET /get-image` | 403 when `AI_GENERATION_ENABLED=0` (cached images still served). |

No database schema change: plans live in the lesson JSON; references use the Phase 3
`asset_references` table; descriptions and keywords live in the existing `assets.details` JSON.

### Files changed
- Created: `visuals.py`, `visuals.js`, `tests/test_visuals.py`, `tests/visuals.test.js`,
  `tests/visuals_browser_check.mjs`.
- Modified: `server.py`, `assets.py`, `index.html`, `tests/backend_env.py` (AI off for all backend
  tests), `tests/mascot_browser_check.mjs` (stubs the new plan call like the other lesson APIs).

### Tests
- `tests/test_visuals.py` (27): the 24 required cases — explicit selection, private and shared
  access, exact/concept and keyword (not file name) matches, no match, procedural, Manim, AI
  fallback, AI disabled (request and server; generators 403), old URL lessons, asset:ID,
  video_asset_id, manim_asset_id, uploaded_image_assets, side_panel.video_asset_id, deleted /
  failed / missing assets (reported, not replaced), consistent plans (earlier match kept),
  planning never calls a generator (each patched to fail if called), 600 assets × 60 scenes

### Results
Final clean QA run (after all fixes, server restarted fresh, all browser tests silent):

| Suite | Result |
|---|---|
| Static checks (6 inline scripts, 5 JS modules, 9 Python modules compile) | pass |
| Backend unit (`unittest`; 39 earlier + 27 new) | **66/66** |
| Frontend unit (`node --test`; 50 earlier + 7 new) | **57/57** |
| Phase 1 mascot browser check | **27/27** |
| Phase 4 visual router browser check | **24/24** |
| Phase 3 Asset Library browser check | **19/19** |
| Phase 2 export end-to-end | **29/30** — failed: "Aadhi never still for 1 s" (1.73 s) |
| Export end-to-end with library assets | **34/35** — failed: same check (2.68 s) |

The failing check is the Phase 3 smoothness metric on the recorded mascot (see Phase 3 Known
limitations); every mandatory flow item passed in both export runs (record, upload, persist,
refresh, preview, seek, download, cancelled share, second-account isolation, VP9 + Opus, narration,
image, chart, Manim, AI video, quiz, final scene, order). In the regression pass before the clean
run: export 29/30 (same check, 2.88 s), library-asset export 33/35 (same check, and once the
export preview played only 0.4 s of its 1.5 s window; it passed in the clean run).

AI / provider calls during all Phase 4 testing: **none**. Backend tests run with
`AI_GENERATION_ENABLED=0` and generators patched to fail; browser tests intercept
`/generate-ai-video` and `/get-image` in the browser; the Phase 2/3 fixtures use local media only.

### Issues found and fixed during the phase
- The lesson start preloaded an AI image for every image panel before anything was decided; it now
  runs after planning and only for images the plan and setting allow.
- A scene's old `video_url` / `manim_video_url` could override the plan (e.g. a stale link after
  the router chose again); the page now follows the plan whenever a scene has one.
- The shared "no Aadhi" background still matched "Aadhi the mascot" (mascot words applied to it,
  and "aadhi" in its file name); hidden-placement stills no longer carry Aadhi words and negated
  words are ignored.
- Skipped view transitions (a newer scene change, e.g. an export restarting the lesson) surfaced
  as an unhandled page error; now handled.
- The new setting first made the setup card taller than a 720-px screen (its top buttons went off
  screen); it now shares a row with the TTS engine.

### Known limitations
- Matching is word-based: an upload is found only through words in its metadata. AI videos
  generated by the app carry their prompt and shared Aadhi assets their roles; uploads need a
  description or keywords, entered in the Asset Library panel (added by the follow-up below).
- Side-panel visuals are never AI videos (a still image is generated instead when nothing
  matches). AI images still come from Pollinations; the provider name is not configurable
  (Phase 8).
- The AI Visuals setting is per browser (localStorage), not per account or lesson.
- An unusable explicit asset shows a "Visual unavailable" card; replacing it (generate a new AI
  video / re-render from the scene's Manim code) is a button, never automatic.
- Plans carry 12-hour signed links; they are refreshed at every presentation start and export.
- Test environment: the recorded-mascot smoothness metric of the Phase 2 export test and, once,
  the export-preview playback timing are sensitive to CPU load; the machine ran other projects'
  jobs (ML training, a pytest suite) during testing. See Results.

### Deferred to Later Phases
- **Phase 5 (AI caching):** generated media is reused only through the library match on its
  prompt; no persistent generation cache or pending/async generation.
- **Phase 6 (Visual review):** only a source badge and a debug trace; no per-scene picker or
  override UI.
- **Phase 8 (Multi-provider AI):** `AiGenerationRule` names the provider from the server's
  current pipeline (veo / ltx / manual / offline, pollinations for images); adding providers
  plugs in there.
- **Phase 10 (Manim sandbox):** Manim is chosen only for scenes that carry code, through the
  unchanged `/render` pipeline.

### Follow-up — asset description & keyword editing
Uploads could only be matched by the router once described, and describing needed an API call.
The Asset Library panel now edits an asset's description and keywords, through the existing
`PATCH /api/assets/{id}` (no new endpoint, table or storage: `assets.details`).

- **Panel (`assets.js`):** the detail pane shows *Description* and *Keywords* (as chips) under
  the file facts; your own undescribed assets say "Not described yet…". **Edit metadata** (own,
  non-deleted assets only, once the detail has loaded) opens a small form — description textarea
  with a character counter, comma-separated keywords, **Save** / **Cancel**. Save sends one PATCH
  and updates the open detail and the list's copy in place ("✓ Metadata saved"; nothing is
  reloaded); Cancel and Escape discard the edit (Escape no longer closes the library while
  editing). A newly uploaded (or re-uploaded, still undescribed) picture or video opens the
  editor automatically with the cursor in the description; audio does not (lessons only pick
  images and videos automatically). Shared Aadhi assets and other users' assets show no editor.
- **Validation (same rules in `assets.js` and `assets.py`):** both fields optional; whitespace
  runs collapsed and trimmed; keywords split on commas, empty entries dropped, repeats dropped
  case-insensitively (first spelling kept); description ≤ 1000 characters, ≤ 30 keywords, each
  ≤ 60 characters. The page explains a problem before saving; the server answers 422 with the
  same wording and changes nothing (previously it silently cut descriptions at 500 and keywords at
  40 characters, and kept "Bridge" and "bridge" as two keywords). An empty field clears it; other
  details (e.g. a generated video's prompt) are kept.
- **Access:** own asset → editable; another user's asset → 404; shared system asset → 403 (and no
  editor); deleted asset → 404; no login → 401.
- **Router:** unchanged. A test uploads a clip, confirms it is not matched, describes it
  ("Bridge carrying heavy traffic"; bridge, traffic, load, stress) and confirms the scene "Show a
  bridge experiencing heavy traffic load" now selects it (relevance 0.67 in the backend test; 0.84
  in the browser check with its fuller description).

Files: `assets.js` (API `update`, keyword/description helpers, editor), `assets.py` (validation
and tidying in the existing PATCH), `index.html` (editor styles), `tests/test_assets.py` (+5),
`tests/test_visuals.py` (+1), `tests/assets.test.js` (+4), `tests/assets_browser_check.mjs`
(+7 checks, 1 extended: editor after upload, cancel, Escape, save in place, shown again when
reopened, router picks the described upload, shared and foreign assets not editable).

The export failure is the same pre-existing smoothness metric as in the Phase 3 and Phase 4 runs

## Phase 5 — AI Media Cache, Deduplication & Reuse

### Objective
Generate once, reuse many times: an AI image or video is never generated again when an equivalent,
valid, generated asset already exists for the user. The cache reuses the Phase 3 library assets
themselves (no second media store) and sits after the Phase 4 router's decision that AI is needed.

### Audit (before Phase 5)
| Path | Provider | Stored | Registered | "Cache" |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `POST /generate-ai-video` (preview Generate, export prep in "all" mode) | Veo `veo-2.0-generate-001` (16:9, ALLOW_ADULT), LTX `Lightricks/LTX-Video` (1280×704, 257 frames, 25 fps, 60 steps, guidance 4.5, seed 42, negative prompt), or manual | `static_videos/ai_video_<sha256(prompt)[:16]>.mp4` | yes (`ai-video`, details.prompt) | file exists by prompt only — shared by all users and all providers; the AI kill switch ran first, so nothing cached was usable with AI off; a regenerate would have overwritten the file lessons use |
| `GET /get-image` (side images via the page's preloader) | Pollinations (800×1200, nologo, random seed; fallback: subject image) | `images/<md5(prompt)>.jpg` | no (no login on the route) | by prompt only; a fallback image was cached as if it were the requested one |
| Visual router (Phase 4) | — | — | — | plans AI, never generates; export and preview call the endpoints above |
No Gemini image calls exist (Gemini is only used for lesson scripts and Veo); GIF search and Manim
are not AI generation.

### Architecture
```
VISUAL ROUTER (unchanged matching) ── AI needed? ──► AI CACHE STEP (reuse_cached_generations)
                                                        │ one query for the lesson
                                        HIT: plan "cached" (asset + link, no generation, works with AI off)
                                        MISS: plan "requires_generation" as before
page / export ──► POST /generate-ai-video | /generate-ai-image
                    identity ─► generation hash ─► lookup (own entries, then shared "system")
                    HIT  ─► existing asset (no provider call, even with AI_GENERATION_ENABLED=0)
                    MISS ─► kill switch ─► lock(scope, hash) ─► recheck ─► provider ─► register asset
                            ─► record cache entry ─► release ─► asset
```
- `ai_cache.py` (new): canonical identity, hashing, `PROVIDER_SETTINGS` (the values the generators
  send — used by both the identity and the provider calls, so they cannot drift), lookups, entries,
  the lock and counters.
- **Identity** = `{v, media_type, provider, model, prompt, parameters}` — only settings the code
  really sends: Veo aspect ratio + person generation; LTX negative prompt, width, height, frames,
  fps, steps, guidance, seed; Pollinations width, height, nologo; manual: none. The Pollinations
  seed is random per call, so it is provenance, not identity. `v` versions the scheme.
- **Canonicalization:** text → Unicode NFC, line endings unified, whitespace runs collapsed,
  trimmed; case and punctuation kept (they can change the output). JSON with sorted keys and no
  insignificant whitespace (UTF-8 kept); lists keep their order. **Generation hash** = SHA-256 of
  that JSON — separate from the file's content hash (`assets.sha256`, unchanged): two different
  requests whose files happen to be identical are two entries on one asset.
- **Database:** new tables `ai_generations` (generation_hash, scope_key, asset_id, media_type,
  provider, model, identity JSON, forced, created_at; index `(scope_key, generation_hash)`) and
  `ai_generation_locks` (key, created_at). Created by `create_all` at startup; existing tables
  unchanged, no migration. Asset details gain `generation` provenance `{hash, provider, model,
  media_type, parameters[, seed]}` next to `prompt`; no secrets.
- **Lookup query:** `ai_generations ⟕ assets WHERE generation_hash IN (…) AND scope_key IN
  ('user:<id>', 'system') ORDER BY created_at DESC` (own entries first) — one indexed query per
  request or per lesson plan; a hit must be `ready`, of the requested kind, usable by the user, with
  a readable file and matching provider/model; otherwise it is a miss (a missing file marks the
  asset failed, as elsewhere). Lessons are never changed by a lookup.
- **Scope (Option A):** entries are private to the user who generated them; `system` entries are
  shared with everyone (the mechanism exists; nothing creates them automatically yet). A user never
  receives another user's generation — they generate their own. Two independent guards: the scope
  filter in the query and the library's access check on every hit (the privacy test fails only when
  both are removed).
- **Force regenerate:** `force_regenerate: true` skips the lookup (the kill switch still applies),
  writes a new file / asset and a new entry (`forced`); the earlier asset, its file and lesson
  references stay exactly as they were; later identical requests reuse the newest version. Not
  available in the manual workflow (there the user replaces the file).
- **Concurrency:** identical requests take a row in `ai_generation_locks` (primary key scope +
  hash). The first generates; the others poll (0.25 s), re-check the cache and reuse its asset.
  Works across threads, event loops and processes sharing the database; a lock older than
  `AI_CACHE_LOCK_MINUTES` (30) is treated as abandoned. Forced regenerations are not serialized.
- **Files:** every generated video gets a new file `ai_video_<hash16>_<rand8>.mp4` in
  `static_videos/` (never overwriting a file a lesson uses); AI images are registered into the
  library's content-addressed store (`assets/blobs`, served by signed links, not public). Manual
  workflow: one deterministic file name per user and request (`ai_video_<sha256(user|hash)[:16]>.mp4`).
- **Kill switch:** cache hits (endpoint and plan) keep working with `AI_GENERATION_ENABLED=0`;
  misses answer the existing 403 and never reach a provider.
- **Visual router:** matching untouched; after routing, AI plans (and "AI needed but off" plans)
  are checked against the cache in one batch; hits become `selection: "cached"`, `cache_hit: true`
  with the asset and a signed link. The library match on a generated asset's prompt can reuse it
  even earlier (same asset).
- **Preview / export:** both plan and generate through the same identity, so preview-then-export
  and export-then-preview end on one asset; the chosen asset is referenced by each lesson (Phase 4
  plan references), one file, one entry, many references.
- **Stand-in provider (tests only):** `AI_FAKE_PROVIDER=1` makes both providers a local ffmpeg
  stand-in (`fake`, its own identities) and makes the real providers refuse to run.

### API
| Endpoint | Change |
|---|---|
| `POST /generate-ai-video` | optional `force_regenerate`; answers add `cache_hit`, `generated` (and keep `status`, `video_url`, `asset_id`, `cached` = `cache_hit`); 400 for an empty prompt; manual answers carry the per-user file name |
| `POST /generate-ai-image` (new, login) | `{prompt, subject_name?, force_regenerate?}` → `{status, url, image_url, asset_id, cache_hit, generated[, fallback]}`; 403 on a miss with AI off |
| `GET /api/ai-cache/stats` (new, login) | counters since start: lookups, hits, misses, generated, bypassed, waited, plan_hits, provider calls; no prompts |
| `POST /api/visuals/plan` | plans may be `selection: "cached"` with `cache_hit: true` |
| `GET /get-image` | unchanged, kept for older pages (unauthenticated, prompt-only file cache, not registered); the current page no longer uses it |

### Frontend
- `visuals.js`: `AiMediaApi` (the two generators; `force` sends `force_regenerate`),
  `generationStatus` ("♻ Reused existing video" / "✨ Generated new video"), badges show
  "(reused)" for cached plans, `cache_hit` kept in stored plans.
- `index.html`: the AI video scene and export preparation use `AiMediaApi`; a small status label
  appears for 4 s after a generation (never while recording); side images come from
  `/generate-ai-image` (one request per prompt at a time) and cached plans' links; the scene list
  has "↻ New AI version" on AI-video scenes (confirmation first; hidden when AI visuals are off).
- `assets.js`: "AI image" source label.

### Files changed
- Created: `ai_cache.py`, `tests/test_ai_cache.py`, `tests/ai_cache_browser_check.mjs`.
- Modified: `models.py`, `server.py`, `visuals.py`, `visuals.js`, `index.html`, `assets.py`,
  `assets.js`, `tests/visuals.test.js`, `tests/mascot_browser_check.mjs` and
  `tests/visuals_browser_check.mjs` (both also stub/intercept the new image endpoint).

### Tests
- `tests/test_ai_cache.py` (19): the 24 required cases — miss then hit, no provider call on a hit,
  different prompt / provider / model / media type / duration / resolution / aspect ratio, force
  regenerate and the kept old asset, deleted / failed / missing assets, another user's private
  generation never reused, shared entries reused, kill switch with a hit and a miss, one asset for
  three lessons, content hash vs generation hash, three concurrent identical requests → one
  generation, preview-then-export and export-then-preview — plus canonicalization, AI images as
  library assets, the fallback image not cached as the request, the manual workflow, validation and
  counters, an abandoned lock, and one indexed query for 40 lookups among 500 foreign entries.
  Providers are replaced by local fakes; any network call fails the test. Mutation-checked (lock,
  status check, new file per generation, scope + access check).
- `tests/visuals.test.js` (+4): cache-hit / generated / older-server responses, status text,
  "(reused)" labels and stored plans, force regenerate request bodies, readable errors.
- `tests/ai_cache_browser_check.mjs` (11 checks, silent, real Chrome, its own throwaway server with
  the stand-in providers): lesson A plans AI video + image → image generated automatically and
  "Generate AI video" clicked → one provider call each, "Generated new video" shown; lesson B with
  the same visuals → same asset IDs, no generator request, no provider call; the router's cache
  step and the generators both report cache hits; "New AI version" → new asset, exactly one
  request with `force_regenerate` and one provider call, old version kept and playable.

### Results
Full regression (server restarted with Phase 5; all browser tests silent):

| Suite | Result |
|---|---|
| Static checks (6 inline scripts, 5 JS modules, 10 Python modules compile) | pass |
| Backend unit (`unittest`; 72 earlier + 19 new) | **91/91** |
| Frontend unit (`node --test`; 61 earlier + 4 new) | **65/65** |
| Phase 1 mascot browser check | **27/27** |

Every export flow item passed. The failing check is the recorded-mascot smoothness metric that has

AI provider calls during Phase 5 testing: **none** (backend: fakes, network blocked; browser: the
stand-in providers of a throwaway server, real providers refusing to run; the earlier browser
checks stub or intercept both generators).

### Known limitations
- Private per-user cache: two users asking for the same visual each generate once. Shared reuse
  exists only through `system` entries, which nothing creates automatically yet.
- AI videos generated before Phase 5 have no generation identity, so they are not cache hits;
  the visual router still reuses them through the library match on their prompt.
- The manual workflow's file name is now per user and request (the page shows it as before).
- The lock is database-backed with polling; a waiting request keeps its HTTP request open while
  the first generates; if that generation fails, a waiting request tries itself. Counters are per
  process and reset on restart.
- After a forced regeneration, later identical requests reuse the newest version.
- `/get-image` stays for older pages outside the cache.
- Test environment: the Phase 2 export end-to-end's recorded-mascot smoothness metric still fails
  (see Results). A silent probe this phase showed it is not caused by the silent audio setup: with
  Chrome's own mute and no `--disable-audio-output` the mascot clips stalled the same way while the
  tab was captured at 60 fps VP9 in headless Chrome. Recording optimization is Phase 7.

### Deferred to Later Phases
- **Phase 6:** a review UI for cached/generated media (only a status label, a badge and "New AI
  version" exist).
- **Phase 7:** recording cost (60 fps VP9 capture) behind the export smoothness metric.
- **Phase 8:** providers plug into `PROVIDER_SETTINGS` and the two provider functions.
- **Phase 9:** unused cached assets are identifiable (`ai_generations` + zero references) but
  nothing is cleaned up; stale lock rows are only taken over, not swept.

### Final status
**IMPLEMENTED — NOT CLAIMED COMPLETE.** The AI media cache, its API, the router step, the page
integration and all Phase 5 tests are in place and passing, and Phases 1, 3 and 4 pass in full. The
Phase 2 export end-to-end still fails its recorded-mascot smoothness metric (pre-existing since
Phase 3, not caused by Phase 5, cause traced to 60 fps VP9 tab capture in headless Chrome — Phase 7),
so per the Phase 5 instructions the phase is not declared complete while that suite is not fully
passing. No AI provider was called during testing. Nothing was committed, pushed or deployed.

## Phase 6 — Visual Review, Approval & Scene-Level Visual Control

### Objective
Let the user see the visual chosen for every scene before exporting, understand why it was chosen,
and keep it, replace it, remove it or generate a new AI version — on top of the Phase 4 router
(initial choice) and the Phase 5 AI cache (reuse), never replacing them.

### Audit (before Phase 6)
- Lessons are generated in the page (`/generate-script`), kept in the `slides` array, backed up to
  localStorage and saved to the server as snapshots (`/save-history` → `projects.json_data`);
  `/?project_id=N` reloads one (`GET /api/projects/{id}` → `openSavedProject`).
- Plans (`scene.visual_plan.main/side`) come from `POST /api/visuals/plan` at presentation start and
  in export preparation; scenes render from them (Phases 4–5). Library links are resolved by
  `/api/assets/resolve`; cached AI media arrives as `selection: "cached"` plans.
- The only scene editing was the script editor (JSON) and the "Presentation Overview" background
  picker; nothing let the user choose, approve or remove a visual. The full-script editor could
  not even apply (it reassigned a `const` array).

### Architecture
```
LESSON ─► VISUAL ROUTER ─► PLAN ─► VISUAL REVIEW (review.js) ─► KEEP / CHANGE / REMOVE / NEW AI VERSION
                                          │
                                          ▼ POST /api/visuals/review (validates, writes the saved lesson)
                                scene.visual_review.<slot> = {status, asset_id?, source?, fingerprint, reviewed_at}
                                          │
             router rule 0: ReviewDecisionRule (before the explicit-asset rule) ─► preview = reload = export
```
- **State model** per visual slot (main / side): *pending* (no entry: the router's choice, not an
  error), *approved* (kept; stores the asset when the plan had one), *changed* (replaced by a chosen
  asset), *removed* (no visual). Every plan carries `review_status` and, when the scene's visual
  request changed after the decision, `review_stale`. New plan selections: `reviewed`, `approved`,
  `removed`.
- **ReviewDecisionRule** (visuals.py) runs first: *removed* → no visual (never re-added); *changed*
  → the chosen asset (if unusable: "Visual unavailable", never a substitute); *approved* with an
  asset → that asset while usable (else routed again, pending); approved without an asset →
  routing reproduces it deterministically.
- **Fingerprint:** a hash of what the scene asks the slot to show (main: type, prompt, Manim / SVG /
  p5 code, visual intent; side: title, side panel without produced media fields, visual intent). A
  generated video or resolved link does not change it; editing the prompt, chart data or picture
  request does. Decision (lesson regeneration, Step 22): a regenerated lesson is a new set of
  scenes without review data, so everything is pending; after a scene is edited an *approval* no
  longer applies (back to pending, flagged), while an explicit *changed* or *removed* choice is
  kept and flagged ("the scene changed after this choice") — a user's choice is never dropped
  silently.
- **Persistence:** the endpoint writes the decision, the scene's recomputed plans and (optionally)
  the page's current copy of the scene (e.g. a video generated in the preview) into the saved lesson
  in place (no new history snapshot), then re-records asset references. Opening the review saves an
  unsaved lesson first; the page mirrors every decision in `slides` and its local backup.
- **Access:** choosing uses the library's own check (own or shared asset; another user's → 404,
  deleted / missing / failed → 409, wrong kind → 422 — a main visual must be a video); the router
  applies `usable_asset` again at every plan, so an edited lesson cannot show another user's asset.
  Shared Aadhi assets can be chosen. Only the lesson's owner can review it.
- **Asset Library integration:** "Choose from Asset Library" opens the existing panel in *pick
  mode* (only pictures/videos the slot can show, "Use this visual", no delete; uploads still work).
  The library search now also matches descriptions, keywords and generated media prompts (the
  `details` JSON; no new index). The chosen asset is never modified; references are recorded
  (`scenes[i].visual_review.<slot>`).
- **AI cache integration:** "Generate AI video/image" for a planned AI visual goes through the
  normal generator (cache first — a hit makes nothing new) and keeps the result as *approved*;
  "Generate new AI version" sends Phase 5 `force_regenerate` (new asset; the old one stays in the
  library and in other lessons) and marks the scene *changed*. Cached visuals show "♻ Reused
  existing visual". Opening or navigating the review never generates.
- **Export:** export preparation replans the lesson, so removed visuals are neither generated nor
  rendered (no AI video, Manim render or side image for them), chosen/approved assets are used, and
  pending visuals behave as before; approving every scene is not required.
- **AI off:** cached and existing assets stay usable and previewable, Asset Library choices and
  built-in/Manim visuals work; the generate actions are disabled with an explanation, and the
  server's 403 is shown if reached.

### UI
- Entry points: 🖼 in the control bar and "Review the visuals of this lesson" on a saved lesson's
  start card. The panel reuses the Asset Library frame: summary ("12 visuals · ✓ 8 approved · ● 3
  need review · ✎ 1 changed · — 1 without visual"), filters (Needs review, Approved, Changed, No
  visual, AI, Asset Library, Built-in, Manim), the scene list with status chips (icon + words), and
  the detail: scene number, title, purpose (subtitle or first narration sentence), preview (image /
  muted video with controls / a Chart.js mini chart / a described placeholder — "Not generated yet",
  "No visual for this scene", "Visual unavailable"), source in words, status, "Why" (the router's
  reason in plain words), notes, actions [Keep] [Change ▾: Choose from Asset Library · Generate
  (new) AI version · Back to automatic choice] [Remove / Restore automatic choice], navigation
  [‹ Previous] [Next ›] [Next needing review] [Show in lesson]. `?visualDebug=1` adds source,
  reason, asset ID and cache status.
- Keyboard-reachable buttons with visible focus, labels, alt text on image previews, `aria-live`
  status, status never by colour alone; stacks on phones without horizontal scrolling.
- Unsaved changes: leaving a scene while its decision is still being saved asks "Discard this
  change?"; the Asset Library's description editor now asks the same before an edit is thrown away
  (switching assets, filtering, closing).
- The scene list (info panel) badges add the review status (e.g. "🗂 Asset Library · ✎ Changed").

### API
| Endpoint | Change |
|---|---|
| `POST /api/visuals/review` (new, login) | `{project_id, scene_index, slot: main\|side, action: keep\|choose\|remove\|reset, asset_id?, scene?, allow_ai_generation?}` → `{review, plans}`; 404 unknown lesson / scene / slot / asset (incl. other users'), 409 unusable asset, 422 bad slot / action / kind / missing asset_id; never generates |
| `POST /api/visuals/plan` | plans carry `review_status`, `review_stale`; selections `reviewed`, `approved`, `removed` |
| `GET /api/assets?q=` | also matches description, keywords and generated media prompts |
| `GET /review.js` | the page module |

No database change: decisions live in the lesson JSON; references use `asset_references`.

### Files changed
- Created: `review.js`, `tests/test_visual_review.py`, `tests/review.test.js`,
  `tests/visual_review_browser_check.mjs`.
- Modified: `visuals.py`, `visuals.js`, `assets.py`, `assets.js`, `index.html`, `server.py`,
  `tests/assets.test.js`.

### Tests
- `tests/test_visual_review.py` (19): the 20 required cases — pending start, Keep → approved,
  choose → changed, Remove → removed, a chosen asset surviving replanning (better match, a
  different `video_asset_id`, library matching off), removed / approved / changed surviving export,
  save and reload, another user's asset refused (and ignored if written into the lesson), a shared
  asset chosen, a missing chosen asset reported and recovered (another choice, back to automatic),
  AI off with a cached asset (kept) and a refused new generation, a new version as a new asset with
  the old one kept for another lesson, the cached generation reused, review actions never
  generating, two scenes sharing an asset, lessons without review data — plus edits making an
  approval pending but keeping explicit choices, the page's scene copy saved, validation, and the
  library search by description/keywords. Mutation-checked (rule order, staleness, access check).
- `tests/review.test.js` (12) and `tests/assets.test.js` (+1): statuses, labels and reasons,
  previews, Keep / Change / Remove / reset, a failed save, new AI version (force) and generating
  the planned visual (cache hit wording), AI off, provider failure and manual workflow,
  navigation and filters, the unsaved-decision question, the API client, unsaved description edits.
- `tests/visual_review_browser_check.mjs` (17 checks, silent, real Chrome, throwaway server with
  the stand-in providers): the seven required scenarios — initial review (all pending, nothing
  generated), Keep (approved after reload), Change via the Asset Library found by its description
  (same asset after reload), Remove (no visual after reload), AI cache (one generation, then a cache
  hit), new AI version (one provider call, old asset kept and playable), export (no warnings; the
  recording showed the new AI version, the chart, the chosen clip and no visual for the removed
  scene; no generation; the downloaded file's pixels show the chosen orange clip) — plus the phone
  layout.

### Results
Full regression (server restarted with Phase 6; all browser tests silent):

| Suite | Result |
|---|---|
| Static checks (6 inline scripts, 6 JS modules, 10 Python modules compile) | pass |
| Backend unit (`unittest`; 91 earlier + 19 new) | **110/110** |
| Frontend unit (`node --test`; 65 earlier + 13 new) | **78/78** |
| Phase 1 mascot browser check | **27/27** |
| Phase 3 Asset Library browser check | **26/26** |
| Phase 4 visual router browser check | **24/24** |
| Phase 5 AI cache browser check | **11/11** |
| Phase 6 Visual Review browser check | **17/17** |
| Phase 2 export end-to-end | **30/30** |
| Export end-to-end with library assets | **35/35** |

The Phase 2 recorded-mascot smoothness metric, failing since the end of Phase 3, passes in both
export runs after the start-card fix above: the card's full-screen blur, recorded over the lesson,
was what made the mascot area look still. No recording setting was changed. (The runs still note
the Phase 1 fallback covering a stalled clip once or twice under headless recording load.)

AI provider calls during Phase 6 testing: **none** (backend: fakes or failing stubs, network
blocked; browser: the stand-in providers of throwaway servers, real providers refusing to run; the
earlier browser checks stub or intercept both generators).

### Issues found and fixed during the phase
- **Exports started from a saved lesson's start card recorded the whole lesson under that card**
  (dimmed, blurred, "▶ Play Video" on top) — visible in the Phase 2 end-to-end's own frames since
  the export test began starting from that card in Phase 3; its checks read the page, not the
  pixels. The export's play step now hides the card (a UI fix in the play hook; no recording
  setting, frame rate, codec or capture option was changed). Found by this phase's pixel check.
- The full-script editor assigned to the `const slides` array and could never apply; it now
  replaces the contents in place (the review, export and cache share that array).
- A generated video chosen as a new version was labelled "Asset Library"; chosen or approved AI
  assets keep their "AI video / AI image" label.

### Known limitations
- "Change" offers the Asset Library, AI generation (where the scene has an AI request) and the
  automatic choice; switching a slot to another built-in renderer type is not offered.
- Built-in visuals other than charts are described in the panel rather than drawn; "Show in
  lesson" shows them in the running lesson.
- Removing a side visual shows the lesson's concept map (the normal panel of scenes without one).
- Keeping a planned, not yet generated AI visual approves the plan; it is generated later as
  before (by the AI Visuals setting or Generate).
- Board images inside a scene's HTML are not reviewed (only the main and side visuals).
- Reviewing needs a saved lesson (opening the review saves an unsaved one first).

### Deferred to Later Phases
- **Phase 7:** recording performance (60 fps VP9 capture, the occasional stalled clip the Phase 1
  fallback covers) is untouched; the smoothness metric itself now passes (see Results).
- **Phase 8:** more providers for "new AI version" plug into Phase 5's provider settings.
- **Phase 9:** unused review-chosen or regenerated assets are identifiable but not cleaned up.

### Final status
**COMPLETE.** Visual Review, its API, the router rule, the page integration and all Phase 6 tests are
in place and passing; every Phase 1–5 suite passes in full, including both Phase 2 export runs.
No AI provider was called during testing. Nothing was committed, pushed or deployed.
