  or copy buttons outside the debug view).
- **Earlier checks adapted** only where the behaviour deliberately changed (the provider line debug-only, the settings
  in the Settings dialog, plain words), each keeping or strengthening what it verified; the export-with-assets check now
  compares its frames by content (two different frames once had the same byte size).
- **The Phase 21 check** (`tests/phase21_browser_check.mjs`): Home, Create from a document, notes and a lesson file and
  their errors (no lesson writer, empty notes, Stop writing, a failed classic generation); the stage rail, "Next step" /
  "Still to do", continuing after a reload and from Home; the editor and "Back to the Studio"; Visual Review's origin
  line and the quality headline; the four Phase 17 styles with the product colours unchanged; play, seek and captions at
  1280×720 and 1920×1080 (a two-line caption clear of the name card; the caption lifted above the shown player bar and
  the name card stepping aside; the playing and recorded caption unchanged); the export and its chapters (one
  "Introduction"); the top bar from a lesson; Settings (five groups, Tab kept inside); five viewports (1440×900,
  1280×720, 1024×768, 820×1180, 600×900); a keyboard-only path; focus into and back from every overlay; reduced motion;
  the Library; no page errors, no dialogs, no provider names in normal mode; all silent.

### LLM validation / real providers
**Not done for real.** No text, image, video, presenter or voice provider key exists on this machine;
every check used the stand-in writer, media and voice on throwaway servers (`AI_FAKE_PROVIDER=1`, `FAKE_TTS=1`) or the
development server, which has no keys. Phase 21 changed no provider path; nothing was faked as a real-provider result.

### Visual quality validation (rendered and inspected)
- **The product at five viewports:** the screenshots of the Phase 21 check (start screen, Settings, Studio home, create,
  stages and style, the player bar and its menu, the editor, Videos, the Library at 1440, 1280, 1024, 820 and 600 px)
  were inspected by the orchestrator and by the educator audit (104 screenshots): one design system throughout; no
  controls overlapping at any width (the editor's three columns at 1024, its inspector row at 820, the Studio's stepper
  at 820 and its select at 600); what was found is in "Issues found and fixed" and "Educator audit".
- **Preview and export (a smaller real-video check):** two lessons written, edited, styled, checked, reviewed, previewed
  and exported through the Studio (`tests/studio_browser_check.mjs`: photosynthesis, 12 scenes with presenter, Aadhi's
  intro, a definition, process steps, a diagram, a display formula, Python code, a comparison table, a quiz, takeaways
  and a summary, captions and transitions, one scene edited; Newton's second law in a second style). Preview screenshots
  and frames of the exported video were compared scene by scene: the same layout, presenter, visuals, formula, code and
  caption in both; the two-line caption now sits below the presenter's name card in the video (in Phase 20's frame of
  the same scene it crossed the card); the chapter list has one "Introduction". The narrated paragraph's highlight box
  is identical to Phase 20's (a stage detail noted under Known limitations).

### Issues found and fixed during the phase
- **Captions re-wrapped in every video (review, high):** the first caption fit also changed how captions break into
  lines, so every recorded two-line caption changed, not only the ones too tall for their band. Removed; stage-fit.css
  is pinned to exactly the two fit rules.
- **Focus fell to the page (review, high):** panels moved focus in before the page behind them stopped being inert, so
  the browser dropped it on `<body>`. The shell now restores focus centrally once inert is lifted (into the panel on
  top, or back to the control that opened it).
- **Panels fought the sign-in dialog (review):** a panel's Tab trap and Esc kept working under a dialog opened above it.
  Every panel (Studio, editor, Visual Review, Library, Videos) ignores its keys while inert.
- **The loading overlay blocked the Studio's and the editor's keys (review):** it counted as "another overlay". It is a
  managed panel now and excluded from those checks.
- **Particles missing from reduced-motion videos (review):** skipping them under reduced motion also dropped them from
  that user's exported video. They are always made and hidden only while no export records.
- **Raw Document Assistant errors (review):** every error is now a plain sentence ending "Your original file is safe —
  try again."; the raw text only with `?visualDebug`.
- **A paused lesson started playing (browser check):** choosing a style or presenter, a review decision, closing the
  editor or "Reload this scene" drew the scene again, and a synchronized scene's narration started on its own. Re-draws
  now show the scene still while the lesson is paused.
- **Tab left the Settings dialog (browser check):** Tab and Shift+Tab now wrap among its visible controls.
- **The caption under the player bar (visual QA, browser check):** the opaque bar showed whenever the pointer was on the
  page (and always on touch screens) and covered the caption band — the whole caption on tablets, where it wraps to two
  rows. Fixed as described under the player bar in "Navigation" (the first lift put a paused two-line caption over the
  name card, so the card now steps aside).
- **The bar never stepped aside after Play was clicked (browser check):** the first version kept the bar while the focus
  was in it, and a mouse click leaves the focus on ▶, so the caption stayed lifted and the name card hidden for the
  whole playback. Only keyboard focus (`:focus-visible`) keeps it now.
- **Focus lost after Visual Review (browser check):** opened from the Studio's Review stage, Visual Review closed onto
  the Studio's heading instead of "Open Visual Review": the Studio had redrawn that button meanwhile. The shell now
  finds the same control again.
- **A paused lesson started playing after an edit (full regression, reproduced alone):** in the editor opened from the
  Studio, on a synchronized cinematic scene, two redraws in a row (an undo or redo changes the history and then the
  composition) let the first render's skipped view transition still run its update first and use up the editor's
  one-shot still preview; the newer render then took the narrated path, which marks the lesson as playing. Only the
  scene drawn last narrates now (a replaced render neither speaks nor uses the still preview); normal playback and
  recordings draw one scene after another and are unchanged. A unit test runs the page's own finalize step in that order
  (it failed without the fix: playing, two narrations).
- **The editor redrew itself until the stack overflowed (found while restyling it):** a failing page callback in its top
  bar redrew the editor again and again; a guard now stops the redraw when the callback fails.

### Educator audit (agent Q) and what changed
A read-only audit as a non-technical teacher (104 screenshots, the copy of every panel) found 5 high, 10 medium and 15
low items in the product UI (and four on the stage, out of scope). Most were fixed in a last round (three agents by file
area, the orchestrator for the page and shell); what was left is under Known limitations:
- **The Studio:** "Next step" never points back (a stage after the suggested one says "Still to do: 3 · Visuals &
  presenter →"); the Visuals numbers add up ("○ 1 to prepare · ✓ 0 ready · …", "All 9 pictures and clips ready · nothing
  left to make"); the Review stage says what it counts ("3 visuals to check"; "Presenters and layouts are checked in
  Visual Review too"); in the Classic layout the Style stage says "Classic layout — video styles need the Cinematic
  layout"; the no-writer note says who can fix it; "Choose existing" says it opens Visual Review (and its failure names
  Visual Review); "Made from" names the real source; the paste form's error sits under the box (`aria-invalid`, focus
  moved there) in the normal font; "your document or notes are safe"; style names as on their cards.
- **The Document Assistant:** "Check how Aadhi read your document, then write the lesson."; sentence-case badges ("From
  your document", "Suggested by AI", "Yours", "Edited"); one verdict ("Ready to write the lesson" / "Could be clearer —
  N notes"); "Sections to teach" (unticking leaves a section out; each box labelled); "Things to check (n)" with a Done
  toggle; "Ideas for visuals", "Ideas for quiz questions", "Suggested improvements"; "✓ Saved" as a status; the main
  button "Write the lesson from this".
- **Visual Review, the Library, videos, the editor:** the export's instructions name the tab by its real title ("Aadhi —
  Educational Video Studio"; they said "Aadhi EduEngine"); Visual Review counts scenes and items ("12 scenes · 33 items
  · ● 28 to check · ✓ 0 approved") and names its rows "Presenter" and "Layout" (the "Composition" filter and wording
  became "Layout"); a hand-made AI video's file path only in debug, with what the teacher can do instead; "Library" in
  every panel (no more "Asset Library"; the stage's "No image / No video for this scene" cards and one export warning
  still say "your asset library"); the editor's "Save version" — which saves a new copy in "Your lessons" and goes on in
  the copy — is now "Save as a copy" and says so; a small note at the editor's stage edge that the panels cover part of
  the scene and Preview shows all of it (≤ 1280 px); "Captions on", "Automatic" instead of "Generated", "Aadhi (no
  presenter)" for a Classic scene where Aadhi shows; "Description used to make it" and "✓ Details saved" in the Library.
- **The page and shell:** the player bar's four tools named on wide screens and tablets; "Developer tools…" in its menu
  only with `?visualDebug`; Settings in plain words ("Visuals & AI", "Start the AI video service" with what it does,
  the second voice list only when it applies, no "read from the server's settings" or "kept for compatibility" notes;
  Advanced still names the AI models and the two unavailable GPU options); "click to choose one"; Visual Review's
  heading ring for keyboard users only; no scene transition while the Studio covers the stage (its snapshot showed over
  the Studio after the editor closed); the style panel's Classic sentence, the transitions' reason ("Needs the Cinematic
  layout") and "your Library".

### Known limitations
- **Real providers not exercised:** no text, image, video, presenter or voice provider key exists on this machine; every
  browser check used the stand-in writer, media and voice. Phase 21 changed no provider path.
- **Library pages:** a load lists at most 50 shared Aadhi assets and the user's 200 newest files (a search or a filter
  reaches older ones). When several kinds are asked at once (the default, and while choosing a scene's visual), the
  per-kind requests are merged into one page of 200, so a long run of one kind can still push older items of another off
  the list; there is no "Load more" yet.
- **The editor's stage:** the editor shows the live full-screen stage behind its panels, so at ≤ 1280 px they cover part
  of the scene (at 1024 px most of a presenter on the right); a note at the stage's edge says Preview shows all of it.
  At 701–900 px the timeline's detail tracks are hidden while the inspector is open (a note says so; "Hide inspector"
  brings them back); at ≤ 700 px it is one column without the stage.
- **While the player bar shows, it covers the bottom of the stage** (the caption is lifted above it and the name card
  steps aside; the board's lower edge may be under the bar until it goes). Preview only.
- **Visual Review's debug view** still shows the Phase 8 provider line, model and renderer, as designed.
- The classic one-step generation is kept (Settings → Advanced) and keeps its older look inside its overlay.
- **Inside the Studio and the editor the top bar is covered** (each has its own way back: "Your lessons", Close, "Back
  to the Studio"); Library, Videos and Settings are reached after closing them. Keeping the bar usable above a
  full-screen panel touches the inert / focus model and was left for a later phase.
- **"Save as a copy" has no version history**, and "Your lessons" has no Delete or Rename yet: copies stay in the list.
- **Smaller wording left as it is:** three date formats (Studio, videos, Library); the lesson's name with or without its
  subject; Title Case on the export panel's buttons ("Start Recording", which the browser checks use); the export panel
  titled "Your videos" while exporting; the scene list's dots on the player bar have no keyboard access of their own
  (arrow keys seek); while a text field in the editor has the focus, it shows "Unsaved changes" (an open edit holds the
  autosave, Phase 19); "→ Ready" beside "✓ Video ready" on a finished lesson's earlier stages; the AI service's own
  refusal sentence in a failed picture's card; the Library's default "All except narration", its "Shared Aadhi assets"
  heading and machine file names; "Reload this scene" in the More menu; the top bar's icons without words at 640 px and
  below.
- **On the stage (the video; out of Phase 21's scope):** the narrated paragraph's highlight box is clipped at its lower
  edge (Phases 13 / 16, unchanged); the skill tree's labels are small at 720p; after a seek the code card is empty for a
  moment; at 600 and 820 px the live lesson reflows to portrait and the presenter is cut (the preview only); under
  reduced motion the stage keeps its slow zoom and float.

### Deferred to later phases
- The final sample-video workflow (SAMPLE_KK.mp4), not started, as instructed.
- The editor's stage fitted between its panels (today the panels cover its edges, with a note), the top bar kept usable
  inside the Studio, lesson versions with Delete / Rename in "Your lessons", "Load more" in the Library, a narrow-screen
  editor with a stage and timeline, one date format.

### Final status
**Complete.**
- **Verified:** the navigation (Home · Create · Library · Videos · Settings) from the start screen, the start card and a
  lesson, Home reachable from an open or playing lesson; one design system (`ui.css`) across the shell, Studio, editor,
  Visual Review, Library, Videos, the Document Assistant and Settings; plain words in the normal view (exceptions under
  Known limitations), technical details only behind `?visualDebug`; empty, loading, success and error states that say
  what happened, whether the work is safe and what to do; keyboard use (focus moved in, kept inside and returned for
  every panel; visible focus; named controls); contrast (every text colour of the tokens on every product surface
  measures 6.9–20.1 : 1, the gold buttons' text 12.9 : 1 — a test keeps it at WCAG AA or better); reduced motion for the
  product UI; intentional layouts at desktop, tablet and narrow widths; the Phase 20 known issues: the Studio refined,
  Visual Review's provider line debug-only, the caption kept off the presenter's name card, one "Introduction" chapter,
  the Library's shared assets found again (no data deleted). Preview and export match.
- **Not verified:** any real AI provider (no keys on this machine; every check used the stand-ins).
- **Unchanged:** Phases 1–20's engines and responsibilities; what the exported video records, except the two changes
  designed for it (captions that fit their band; one plain card instead of developer text for a missing visual) and the
  chapter list's possible "Opening".
- **Nothing committed or deployed.** No database schema change; the development database and its assets were kept.
